$$clearledger.cl_idempotency_row_valid(scope, status_code, response_body)$$);\n\n-- ===========================================================================\n-- Trigger functions\n-- ===========================================================================\n\n-- settlements: a new aggregate always starts at version 1.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_settlements_before_insert()\nRETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nBEGIN\n  IF NEW.version IS DISTINCT FROM 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % must be initiated at version 1 (got %)', NEW.settlement_id, NEW.version\n      USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation';\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- settlements: immutable header, +1 version stepping, clearing lifecycle.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_settlements_before_update()\nRETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nBEGIN\n  IF OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % is RECONCILED and terminal', OLD.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.settlement_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.settlement_id\n     OR NEW.account_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.account_id\n     OR NEW.reference IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.reference\n     OR NEW.debit_party IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.debit_party\n     OR NEW.credit_party IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.credit_party\n     OR NEW.created_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.created_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % header columns are immutable', OLD.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.version IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.version + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % version must step from % to %', OLD.settlement_id, OLD.version, OLD.version + 1;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.entry_count IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.entry_count + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % entry_count must step by +1', OLD.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.last_entry_id IS NULL OR NEW.last_entry_id IS NOT DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % update requires a new last_entry_id', OLD.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.cl_transition_ok(OLD.current_status, NEW.current_status) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % illegal status transition % -> %',\n      OLD.settlement_id, OLD.current_status, NEW.current_status;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.updated_at < OLD.updated_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % updated_at must not move backwards', OLD.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- events: contiguous versions, entryId uniqueness, parent coherence, ordering.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_events_before_insert()\nRETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nDECLARE\n  err   text;\n  s     clearledger.settlements%ROWTYPE;\n  d     jsonb;\n  prev  clearledger.events%ROWTYPE;\n  maxv  integer;\n  eid   uuid;\nBEGIN\n  err := clearledger.cl_envelope_error(NEW.payload);\n  IF err IS NOT NULL THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid event envelope: %', err USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation';\n  END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.cl_event_columns_match(NEW.payload, NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id, NEW.aggregate_version,\n        NEW.event_type, NEW.correlation_id, NEW.idempotency_key, NEW.occurred_at) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event columns do not match the envelope' USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation';\n  END IF;\n\n  SELECT * INTO s FROM clearledger.settlements WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % does not exist', NEW.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = 'foreign_key_violation';\n  END IF;\n\n  SELECT COALESCE(MAX(aggregate_version), 0) INTO maxv\n    FROM clearledger.events WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version <> maxv + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event version % for settlement % is not contiguous (expected %)',\n      NEW.aggregate_version, NEW.settlement_id, maxv + 1;\n  END IF;\n\n  d := NEW.payload->'data';\n  IF d->>'accountId' IS DISTINCT FROM s.account_id\n     OR d->>'reference' IS DISTINCT FROM s.reference\n     OR d->>'debitParty' IS DISTINCT FROM s.debit_party\n     OR d->>'creditParty' IS DISTINCT FROM s.credit_party THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event header does not match settlement %', NEW.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF d->>'status' IS DISTINCT FROM s.current_status\n     OR d->>'clearingStage' IS DISTINCT FROM s.current_stage THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event status/stage does not match settlement %', NEW.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  eid := CASE WHEN jsonb_typeof(d->'entryId') = 'string' THEN (d->>'entryId')::uuid END;\n  IF eid IS DISTINCT FROM s.last_entry_id THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event entryId does not match settlement % last_entry_id', NEW.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF (CASE WHEN jsonb_typeof(d->'memo') = 'string' THEN d->>'memo' END) IS DISTINCT FROM s.last_memo THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event memo does not match settlement % last_memo', NEW.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version IS DISTINCT FROM s.version THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event version % does not match settlement version %', NEW.aggregate_version, s.version;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.occurred_at IS DISTINCT FROM s.updated_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event occurred_at must equal settlement updated_at';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version = 1 THEN\n    IF NEW.occurred_at IS DISTINCT FROM s.created_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'initiation event occurred_at must equal settlement created_at';\n    END IF;\n  ELSE\n    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.events e\n                WHERE e.settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id\n                  AND e.event_type = 'LedgerEntryRecorded'\n                  AND e.payload->'data'->>'entryId' IS NOT NULL\n                  AND lower(e.payload->'data'->>'entryId') = lower(d->>'entryId')) THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'entryId % already recorded for settlement %', d->>'entryId', NEW.settlement_id\n        USING ERRCODE = 'unique_violation';\n    END IF;\n    SELECT * INTO prev FROM clearledger.events\n     WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id AND aggregate_version = NEW.aggregate_version - 1;\n    IF NOT FOUND THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'previous event % missing for settlement %', NEW.aggregate_version - 1, NEW.settlement_id;\n    END IF;\n    IF NEW.occurred_at < prev.occurred_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'event occurred_at must not precede the previous event';\n    END IF;\n    IF NOT clearledger.cl_transition_ok(prev.payload->'data'->>'status', d->>'status') THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'illegal status transition % -> %', prev.payload->'data'->>'status', d->>'status';\n    END IF;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_append_only()\nRETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nBEGIN\n  RAISE EXCEPTION '%.% is append-only: % is not permitted', TG_TABLE_SCHEMA, TG_TABLE_NAME, TG_OP;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- outbox: must exactly mirror the committed event, in contiguous order.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_outbox_before_insert()\nRETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nDECLARE\n  e    clearledger.events%ROWTYPE;\n  maxv integer;\n  err  text;\nBEGIN\n  err := clearledger.cl_envelope_error(NEW.payload);\n  IF err IS NOT NULL THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid outbox envelope: %', err USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation';\n  END 'foreign_key_violation';\n  END IF;\n  IF e.settlement_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.settlement_id\n     OR e.aggregate_version IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.aggregate_version\n     OR e.correlation_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.correlation_id\n     OR e.payload IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.payload THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox row for event % does not mirror clearledger.events', NEW.event_id;\n  END IF;\n  SELECT COALESCE(MAX(aggregate_version), 0) INTO maxv\n    FROM clearledger.outbox WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version <> maxv + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox version % for settlement % is not contiguous (expected %)',\n      NEW.aggregate_version, NEW.settlement_id, maxv + 1;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.attempts < 0 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox attempts must be >= 0' ERRCODE = 'check_violation';\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- outbox: delivery / archival lifecycle.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_outbox_before_update()\nRETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nBEGIN\n  IF NEW.seq IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.seq\n     OR NEW.event_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.event_id\n     OR NEW.settlement_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.settlement_id\n     OR NEW.aggregate_version IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.aggregate_version\n     OR NEW.correlation_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.correlation_id\n     OR NEW.payload IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.payload\n     OR NEW.created_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.created_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox envelope columns are immutable (seq %)', OLD.seq;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.attempts < OLD.attempts THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox attempts cannot decrease (seq %)', OLD.seq;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF OLD.published_at IS NULL AND NEW.published_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n    -- publishing\n    IF NEW.attempts <= OLD.attempts THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'publishing outbox seq % requires incrementing attempts', OLD.seq;\n    END IF;\n    IF NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox seq % cannot be archived while being published', OLD.seq;\n    END IF;\n  ELSIF OLD.published_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.published_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n    -- archival / re-archival of a published row\n    IF NEW.published_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.published_at\n       OR NEW.attempts IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.attempts\n       OR NEW.last_error IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_error THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'published outbox seq % delivery columns are immutable', OLD.seq;\n    END IF;\n    IF OLD.archived_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL\n       AND NEW.archived_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.archived_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox seq % archived_at cannot be rewritten without first resetting it', OLD.seq;\n    END IF;\n  ELSIF OLD.published_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.published_at IS NULL THEN\n    -- operational replay\n    IF NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox seq % replay must also reset archived_at', OLD.seq;\n    END IF;\n  ELSE\n    -- still unpublished (e.g. a failed attempt recording last_error)\n    IF NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'unpublished outbox seq % cannot be archived', OLD.seq;\n    END IF;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_outbox_before_delete()\nRETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nBEGIN\n  RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.outbox rows cannot be deleted (seq %)', OLD.seq;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- idempotency_keys: must reference a committed event + outbox row.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_idempotency_before_insert()\nRETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nDECLARE\n  sid uuid;\n  eid uuid;\n  ver integer;\nBEGIN\n  IF NOT clearledger.cl_idempotency_row_valid(NEW.scope, NEW.status_code, NEW.response_body) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid RAISE EXCEPTION 'idempotency record references no outbox row for event %', eid\n      USING ERRCODE = 'foreign_key_violation';\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- DynamoDB projection convergence\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef S(v):\n    return {\"S\": v}\n\n\ndef N(v):\n    return {\"N\": str(int(v))}\n\n\ndef Valkey convergence\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------\nclass Resp:\n    def __init__(self, host, port):\n        self.sock = socket.create_connection((host, port), timeout=15)\n        self.f = self.sock.makefile(\"rb\")\n\n    def cmd(self, *args):\n        out = b\"*%d\\r\\n\" % len(args)\n        for a in args:\n            a = a if isinstance(a, bytes) else str(a).encode()\n            out += b\"$%d\\r\\n%s\\r\\n\" % (len(a), a)\n        self.sock.sendall(out)\n        return self._read()\n\n    def _read(self):\n        line = self.f.readline()\n        t, rest = line[:1], line[1:-2]\n        if t == b\"+\":\n            return rest.decode()\n        if t == b\"-\":\n            raise RuntimeError(rest.decode())\n        if t == b\":\":\n            return int(rest)\n        if t == b\"$\":\n            n = int(rest)\n            if n < 0:\n                return None\n            data = self.f.read(n + 2)\n            return data[:-2]\n        if t == b\"*\":\n            n = int(rest)\n            return None if n < 0 else [self._read() for _ in range(n)]\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"unexpected RESP reply {line!r}\")\n\n\ndef projection_matches(raw, s):\n    try:\n        c = json.loads(raw)\n    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n        return False\n    if not isinstance(c, dict):\n        return False\n    want = {\n        \"settlementId\": s[\"settlement_id\"], \"accountId\": s[\"account_id\"], \"reference\": s[\"reference\"],\n        \"debitParty\": s[\"debit_party\"], \"creditParty\": s[\"credit_party\"], \"status\": s[\"current_status\"],\n        \"clearingStage\": s[\"current_stage\"], \"lastEntryId\": s.get(\"last_entry_id\"),\n        \"lastMemo\": s.get(\"last_memo\"), \"version\": s[\"version\"], \"entryCount\": s[\"entry_count\"],\n    }\n    allowed = set(want) | {\"updatedAt\"}\n    if set(c) - allowed:\n        return False\n    for k, v in want.items():\n        got = c.get(k)\n        if k == \"lastEntryId\" and got is not None and v is not None:\n            if str(got).lower() != str(v).lower():\n                return False\n        elif k == \"settlementId\":\n            if str(got).lower() != str(v).lower():\n                return False\n        elif got != v:\n            return False\n    try:\n        return parse_ts(c[\"updatedAt\"]) == parse_ts(s[\"updated_at\"])\n    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n        return False\n\n\ndef scan_cache(settlements, r):\n    by_id = {s[\"settlement_id\"].lower(): s for s in settlements}\n    cursor, removed, kept = \"0\", 0, 0\n    while True:\n        cursor, keys = r.cmd(\"SCAN\", cursor, \"COUNT\", 1000)\n        cursor = cursor.decode() if isinstance(cursor, bytes) else str(cursor)\n        for kb in keys:\n            k = kb.decode(errors=\"replace\")\n            m = CACHE_KEY_RE.match(k)\n            ok = False\n            if m and m.group(1).lower() in by_id:\n                t = r.cmd(\"TYPE\", kb)\n                ttl = r.cmd(\"TTL\", kb)\n                if t == \"string\" and isinstance(ttl, int) and 0 < ttl <= 90:\n                    raw = r.cmd(\"GET\", kb)\n                    ok = raw is not None and projection_matches(raw, by_id[m.group(1).lower()])\n            if ok:\n                kept += 1\n            else:\n                r.cmd(\"DEL\", kb)\n                removed += 1\n        if cursor == \"0\":\n            break\n    return kept, removed\n\n\ndef token(kind):\n    c = MANIFEST[\"auth\"][\"clients\"][kind]\n    basic = base64.b64encode(f\"{c['client_id']}:{c['client_secret']}\".encode()).decode()\n    req = urllib.request.Request(\n        MANIFEST[\"auth\"][\"token_endpoint\"],\n        data=urllib.parse.urlencode({\"grant_type\": \"client_credentials\", \"scope\": c[\"scope\"]}).encode(),\n        headers={\"Authorization\": f\"Basic {basic}\", \"Content-Type\": \"application/x-www-form-urlencoded\"},\n    )\n    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=15) as resp:\n        return json.loads(resp.read())[\"access_token\"]\n\n\ndef converge_valkey(settlements):\n    cache = MANIFEST[\"cache\"]\n    r = Resp(cache[\"endpoint\"], int(cache[\"port\"]))\n    kept, removed = scan_cache(settlements, r)\n    log(f\"valkey: kept {kept} valid entries, removed {removed} orphan/stale/divergent keys\")\n\n    # Warm the cache through the API so each settlement has a canonical,\n    # TTL-bounded entry written by the application itself.\n    if 0 < len(settlements) <= 400:\n        try:\n            tok = token(\"read\")\n            base = MANIFEST[\"service_url\"].rstrip(\"/\")\n            for s in settlements:\n                req = urllib.request.Request(\n                    f\"{base}/v1/settlements/{s['settlement_id']}\",\n                    headers={\"Authorization\": f\"Bearer {tok}\", \"X-Correlation-Id\": \"deploy-reconcile\"})\n                try:\n                    urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=10).read()\n                except Exception:  # S3 audit archive convergence\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef list_versions(bucket):\n    versions, markers = [], []\n    kw = {\"Bucket\": bucket}\n    while True:\n        r = s3.list_object_versions(**kw)\n        versions.extend(r.get(\"Versions\", []))\n        markers.extend(r.get(\"DeleteMarkers\", []))\n        if not r.get(\"IsTruncated\"):\n            break\n        kw = {\"Bucket\": bucket}\n        if r.get(\"NextKeyMarker\"):\n            kw[\"KeyMarker\"] = r[\"NextKeyMarker\"]\n        if r.get(\"NextVersionIdMarker\"):\n            kw[\"VersionIdMarker\"] = r[\"NextVersionIdMarker\"]\n    return versions, markers\n\n\ndef delete_versions(bucket, objs):\n    objs = [o for o in objs if o.get(\"Key\") is not None]\n    for i in range(0, len(objs), 500):\n        chunk = objs[i:i + 500]\n        try:\n            r = s3.delete_objects(Bucket=bucket, Delete={\"Objects\": chunk, \"Quiet\": True})\n            errs = r.get(\"Errors\", [])\n        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001\n            log(f\"s3: bulk delete failed ({e}); deleting individually\")\n            errs = chunk\n        for o in errs:\n            kw = {\"Bucket\": bucket, \"Key\": o[\"Key\"]}\n            if o.get(\"VersionId\") and o[\"VersionId\"] != \"null\":\n                kw[\"VersionId\"] = o[\"VersionId\"]\n            elif o.get(\"VersionId\") == \"null\":\n                kw[\"VersionId\"] = \"null\"\n            try:\n                s3.delete_object(**kw)\n            except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001\n                log(f\"s3: delete {kw} failed: {e}\")\n\n\ndef validate_batch(key, body, by_event, claimed):\n    m = BATCH_KEY_RE.match(key)\n    if not m:\n        return None, \"non-canonical key\"\n    if hashlib.sha256(body).hexdigest()[:16] != m.group(3):\n        return None, \"digest mismatch\"\n    try:\n        text = body.decode(\"utf-8\")\n    except UnicodeDecodeError:\n        return None, \"not utf-8\"\n    lines = [ln for ln in text.split(\"\\n\") if ln.strip() != \"\"]\n    if not lines:\n        return None, \"empty batch\"\n    seqs = []\n    for ln in lines:\n        try:\n            rec = json.loads(ln)\n        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n            return None, \"invalid json line\"\n        if not isinstance(rec, dict):\n            return None, \"record is not an object\"\n        row = by_event.get(str(rec.get(\"eventId\", \"\")).lower())\n        if row is None:\n            return None, \"unknown event\"\n        if rec != row[\"payload\"]:\n            return None, \"payload mismatch\"\n        seqs.append(int(row[\"seq\"]))\n    if any(b <= a for a, b in zip(seqs, seqs[1:])):\n        return None, \"records not in ascending seq order\"\n    if seqs[0] != int(m.group(1)) or seqs[-1] != int(m.group(2)):\n        return None, \"key seq range mismatch\"\n    if any(s in claimed for s in seqs):\n        return None, \"duplicate events across batches\"\n    return seqs, None\n\n\ndef s3_pass(bucket):\n    \"\"\"One convergence pass. Returns (changes, covered seqs).\"\"\"\n    versions, markers = list_versions(bucket)\n    # Snapshot after listing: every archived record already has a row.\n    rows = pg_json(\"SELECT seq, event_id, payload, published_at, archived_at FROM clearledger.outbox ORDER BY seq\")\n    by_event = {r[\"event_id\"].lower(): r for r in rows}\n\n    by_key = {}\n    for v in versions:\n        by_key.setdefault(v[\"Key\"], []).append(dict(v, _marker=False))\n    for d in markers:\n        by_key.setdefault(d[\"Key\"], []).append(dict(d, _marker=True))\n\n    def batch_sort(k):\n        m = BATCH_KEY_RE.match(k)\n        return (0, int(m.group(1)), k) if m else (1, 0, k)\n\n    to_delete, claimed, changes = [], set(), 0\n    for key in sorted(by_key, key=batch_sort):\n        entries = by_key[key]\n        latest = next((e for e in entries if e.get(\"IsLatest\")), None)\n        keep = None\n        if latest is not None and not latest[\"_marker\"] and key.startswith(\"ledger-audit/\"):\n            try:\n                body = s3.get_object(Bucket=bucket, Key=key, VersionId=latest[\"VersionId\"])[\"Body\"].read()\n            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n                body = s3.get_object(Bucket=bucket, Key=key)[\"Body\"].read()\n            seqs, why = validate_batch(key, body, by_event, claimed)\n            if seqs is not None:\n                claimed.update(seqs)\n                keep = latest\n            else:\n                log(f\"s3: dropping {key}: {why}\")\n        elif latest is None or latest[\"_marker\"]:\n            log(f\"s3: dropping {key}: deleted (delete marker is current)\")\n        else:\n            log(f\"s3: dropping {key}: outside ledger-audit/\")\n        for e in entries:\n            if keep is not Your job is to write the Terraform or OpenTofu configuration, wire up the networking, KMS keys, Cognito OAuth2 scopes, and IAM roles, initialize the PostgreSQL schema and triggers, and provide deployment and teardown scripts that hold up under live traffic and recovery drills.  The application is already compiled into four container images and loaded into the local runtime. Do not rebuild or modify the application binaries. Your work is strictly on the infrastructure, database schema, and operational lifecycle side:  1. **API service (`api_image`)**: Serves the HTTP API (`POST /v1/settlements`, `POST /v1/settlements/{id}/entries`, `GET /v1/settlements/{id}`, `GET /v1/settlements/{id}/ledger`, `POST /v1/admin/projections/{id}/rebuild`, `/health/live`, and `/health/ready`). Deploy this as a container-based **AWS Lambda** function connected to the main SQS queue via an event source mapping (`batch_size = 5`, `ReportBatchItemFailures` enabled) backed by a dead-letter queue (`maxReceiveCount = 4`). 3. **Outbox relay (`relay_image`)**: Polls **Amazon RDS for PostgreSQL** for committed outbox rows (`published_at IS NULL`), publishes them to the main SQS queue, and stamps `published_at`. Deploy this as a container-based **AWS Lambda** function triggered every minute (`rate(1 minute)`) by **EventBridge Scheduler**. 4. **Audit archiver (`archiver_image`)**: Reads published settlement events from PostgreSQL (`published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`) and writes deterministic NDJSON audit batches (`ledger-audit/batch-*.ndjson`) to a private, versioned **Amazon S3** bucket. Deploy this as a container-based **AWS Lambda** function triggered every five minutes (`rate(5 minutes)`) by **EventBridge Scheduler**.  ## Workspace Environment and Contracts  The workspace comes with `terraform`, `tofu`, `aws`, `psql`, `jq`, `curl`, and Python 3 pre-installed. The local AWS control plane (`http://aws:4566`, region `us-east-1`) is configured through `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL` and `/workspace/config/config.json`. Docker is intentionally disabled inside the workspace; provision every cloud resource through Terraform or OpenTofu against the AWS endpoint.  Read the contract files under `/workspace/contracts/` before writing your code:  - `/workspace/contracts/runtime.md`: Container environment variables for the API and each Lambda worker (`DATABASE_URL`, `SQS_QUEUE_URL`, `PROJECTION_TABLE`, `VALKEY_URL`, `CACHE_TTL_SECONDS`, `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE`, `AUDIT_BUCKET`, `AUDIT_PREFIX`, `AUTH_ISSUER`, `AUTH_AUDIENCES`, `AUTH_JWKS_URL`, `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`), the three non-hierarchical Cognito OAuth2 scopes (`clearledger/read`, `clearledger/write`, `clearledger/admin`), and the data-plane reconciliation rules required when `deploy.sh` runs. - `/workspace/contracts/infrastructure.md` and `/workspace/contracts/services/*.md`: Service-by-service infrastructure requirements covering:   - `services/vpc.md`: Two-AZ VPC (`us-east-1a` and `us-east-1b`), public and private subnets, route tables, and four least-privilege security groups (`alb`, `ecs`, `rds`, `valkey`).   - `services/alb.md` and `services/ecs.md`: Application Load Balancer, target group, HTTP listener, ECS cluster (`containerInsights = \"enabled\"`), Fargate task definition, and service configuration.   - `services/rds.md`: RDS PostgreSQL 16 (`db.t4g.micro`) instance settings and the `clearledger` schema (`settlements`, `events`, `outbox`, `idempotency_keys`), `CHECK` / `UNIQUE` / `FOREIGN KEY` constraints, PL/pgSQL triggers, and partial/composite indexes.   - `services/sqs.md`, `services/lambda.md`, and `services/scheduler.md`: Main SQS queue and DLQ parameters, Lambda container workers, SQS event source mapping, and EventBridge Scheduler rules.   - `services/dynamodb.md`, `services/elasticache-valkey.md`, and `services/s3.md`: DynamoDB key schema (`PK`/`SK`), `AccountIndex` GSI (`GSI1PK`/`GSI1SK`), and point-in-time recovery, ElastiCache Valkey 8 (`cache.t4g.micro`), and S3 bucket versioning, KMS encryption, public-access block, and canonical batch key layout.   - `services/cognito.md`, `services/iam.md`, `services/kms.md`, and `services/cloudwatch-logs.md`: Cognito User Pool, resource server, and three client-credentials app clients, the six dedicated IAM roles (`ecs_execution`, `ecs_task`, `projector`, `relay`, `archiver`, `scheduler`) with both least-privilege `Allow` statements and explicit `Effect = \"Deny\"` guardrails, the four customer-managed KMS keys and aliases (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`), and the four CloudWatch Log Groups (`retention_in_days >= 14`). - `/workspace/contracts/openapi.yaml`: HTTP endpoints, request and response bodies, required request headers (`Idempotency-Key`, `X-Correlation-Id`), and response headers (`X-ClearLedger-Source`, `X-ClearLedger-Version`, `X-ClearLedger-Instance`). - `/workspace/contracts/schemas/events.schema.json`: Domain event envelope schema used on SQS and in S3 NDJSON audit batches. - `/workspace/contracts/schemas/manifest.schema.json`: JSON Schema for `/workspace/submission/manifest.json` (`additionalProperties: false` throughout).  Deployment inputs (`resource_prefix`, `region`, `aws_endpoint_url`, `db_name`, `db_username`, `db_password`, and the four container image tags and digests: `api_image` / `clearledger/api:1.0.0`, `projector_image` / `clearledger/projector:1.0.0`, `relay_image` / `clearledger/relay:1.0.0`, `archiver_image` / `clearledger/archiver:1.0.0`) are stored in `/workspace/config/config.json`. Read those values dynamically in `deploy.sh` and `destroy.sh` instead of hardcoding values from the current workspace.  ## Deliverables and Lifecycle Rules  Write your solution under `/workspace/submission/`:  ```text /workspace/submission/ ????????? deploy.sh ????????? destroy.sh ????????? manifest.json ????????? infra/     ????????? *.tf (or *.tofu) ```  - `infra/`: Your Terraform or OpenTofu configuration. All required cloud resources and IAM policies must be declared here and tracked in `/workspace/submission/infra/terraform.tfstate`. Do not create scored cloud resources via imperative `aws` CLI commands in `deploy.sh`. - `deploy.sh` (timeout: 720 seconds, max combined stdout/stderr: 8 MiB):   - Initializes and applies `/workspace/submission/infra` with local state at `/workspace/submission/infra/terraform.tfstate`.   - Idempotently creates the `clearledger` PostgreSQL schema, tables, relational `CHECK` / `UNIQUE` / `FOREIGN KEY` constraints, triggers, and indexes on the RDS instance as specified in `/workspace/contracts/services/rds.md`.   - Exports `/workspace/submission/manifest.json` (max 1 MiB) from your Terraform/OpenTofu outputs matching `/workspace/contracts/schemas/manifest.schema.json`.   - Waits until `GET <service_url>/health/ready` returns HTTP `200`.   - Handles repair and re-apply cleanly: re-running `deploy.sh` must keep the existing RDS instance, DynamoDB table, and S3 bucket intact without losing committed data, fix out-of-band control-plane drift (across SQS/DLQ attributes, EventBridge schedules, CloudWatch Log Group retention, Lambda environment variables, out-of-band inline or attached IAM policies on the six workload roles, and security group egress rules), recreate and re-wire any deleted resources (such as the SQS queues, schedules, or the projector event source mapping), drain unpublished outbox rows (`published_at IS NULL`) through `outbox_relay`, reconcile DynamoDB (`STATE`, `EVENT#*`, and `AccountIndex` GSI attributes) and Valkey cache entries 1-to-1 against `clearledger.settlements` and `clearledger.events` (deleting any orphan partitions or stray items/keys), and reconcile the versioned S3 `ledger-audit/` archive 1-to-1 against `clearledger.outbox` through `audit_archiver` (including purging noncurrent object `Versions` and `DeleteMarkers`) before exiting `0`. - `destroy.sh` (timeout: 900 seconds, max combined stdout/stderr: 8 MiB):   - Tears down all resources created for the active `resource_prefix` while leaving pre-existing baseline (`cl-base-*`) resources untouched.   - Cleans up any out-of-band operational resources or attachments scoped to `<resource_prefix>` (including inline or multi-version customer-managed IAM policies, breakglass IAM roles, non-empty versioned S3 buckets, DynamoDB tables, SQS queues, EventBridge schedules, customer-managed KMS keys/aliases, and `/clearledger/<resource_prefix>` CloudWatch Log Groups) so `terraform.tfstate` has zero remaining managed resources and post-destroy cloud inventory matches the pre-deployment baseline. S    18:50   0:00 bash /opt/venv/bin/opencode --model=anthropic/claude-opus-5-5 run --format=json --thinking --dangerously-skip-permissions -- We need to stand up the AWS infrastructure for **ClearLedger**, an event-sourced interbank clearing and settlement ledger service, against the local AWS control plane (`http://aws:4566`). Your job is to write the Terraform or OpenTofu configuration, wire up the networking, KMS keys, Cognito OAuth2 scopes, and IAM roles, initialize the PostgreSQL schema and triggers, and provide deployment and teardown scripts that hold up under live traffic and recovery drills.  The application is already compiled into four container images and loaded into the local runtime. Do not rebuild or modify the application binaries. Your work is strictly on the infrastructure, database schema, and operational lifecycle side:  1. **API service (`api_image`)**: Serves the HTTP API (`POST /v1/settlements`, `POST /v1/settlements/{id}/entries`, `GET /v1/settlements/{id}`, `GET /v1/settlements/{id}/ledger`, `POST /v1/admin/projections/{id}/rebuild`, `/health/live`, and `/health/ready`). Run this on **ECS Fargate** (`desired_count = 2` across `us-east-1a` and `us-east-1b`) in private subnets behind an internet-facing **Application Load Balancer** (`port 80` HTTP listener forwarding to a target group on `port 8080` with `/health/ready` health checks). 2. **Projector (`projector_image`)**: Consumes domain events from the main **SQS** queue, writes the current settlement projection and ordered ledger event items into **DynamoDB**, and invalidates stale cached projections in **ElastiCache for Valkey**. Deploy this as a container-based **AWS Lambda** function connected to the main SQS queue via an event source mapping (`batch_size = 5`, `ReportBatchItemFailures` enabled) backed by a dead-letter queue (`maxReceiveCount = 4`). 3. **Outbox relay (`relay_image`)**: Polls **Amazon RDS for PostgreSQL** for committed outbox rows (`published_at IS NULL`), publishes them to the main SQS queue, and stamps `published_at`. Deploy this as a container-based **AWS Lambda** function triggered every minute (`rate(1 minute)`) by **EventBridge Scheduler**. 4. **Audit archiver (`archiver_image`)**: Reads published settlement events from PostgreSQL (`published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`) and writes deterministic NDJSON audit batches (`ledger-audit/batch-*.ndjson`) to a private, versioned **Amazon S3** bucket. Deploy this as a container-based **AWS Lambda** function triggered every five minutes (`rate(5 minutes)`) by **EventBridge Scheduler**.  ## Workspace Environment and Contracts  The workspace comes with `terraform`, `tofu`, `aws`, `psql`, `jq`, `curl`, and Python 3 pre-installed. The local AWS control plane (`http://aws:4566`, region `us-east-1`) is configured through `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL` and `/workspace/config/config.json`. Docker is intentionally disabled inside the workspace; provision every cloud resource through Terraform or OpenTofu against the AWS endpoint.  Read the contract files under `/workspace/contracts/` before writing your code:  - `/workspace/contracts/runtime.md`: Container environment variables for the API and each Lambda worker (`DATABASE_URL`, `SQS_QUEUE_URL`, `PROJECTION_TABLE`, `VALKEY_URL`, `CACHE_TTL_SECONDS`, `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE`, `AUDIT_BUCKET`, `AUDIT_PREFIX`, `AUTH_ISSUER`, `AUTH_AUDIENCES`, `AUTH_JWKS_URL`, `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`), the three non-hierarchical Cognito OAuth2 scopes (`clearledger/read`, `clearledger/write`, `clearledger/admin`), and the data-plane reconciliation rules required when `deploy.sh` runs. - `/workspace/contracts/infrastructure.md` and `/workspace/contracts/services/*.md`: Service-by-service infrastructure requirements covering:   - `services/vpc.md`: Two-AZ VPC (`us-east-1a` and `us-east-1b`), public and private subnets, route tables, and four least-privilege security groups (`alb`, `ecs`, `rds`, `valkey`).   - `services/alb.md` and `services/ecs.md`: Application Load Balancer, target group, HTTP listener, ECS cluster (`containerInsights = \"enabled\"`), Fargate task definition, and service configuration.   - `services/rds.md`: RDS PostgreSQL 16 (`db.t4g.micro`) instance settings and the `clearledger` schema (`settlements`, `events`, `outbox`, `idempotency_keys`), `CHECK` / `UNIQUE` / `FOREIGN KEY` constraints, PL/pgSQL triggers, and partial/composite indexes.   - `services/sqs.md`, `services/lambda.md`, and `services/scheduler.md`: Main SQS queue and DLQ parameters, Lambda container workers, SQS event source mapping, and EventBridge Scheduler rules.   - `services/dynamodb.md`, `services/elasticache-valkey.md`, and `services/s3.md`: DynamoDB key schema (`PK`/`SK`), `AccountIndex` GSI (`GSI1PK`/`GSI1SK`), and point-in-time recovery, ElastiCache Valkey 8 (`cache.t4g.micro`), and S3 bucket versioning, KMS encryption, public-access block, and canonical batch key layout.   - `services/cognito.md`, `services/iam.md`, `services/kms.md`, and `services/cloudwatch-logs.md`: Cognito User Pool, resource server, and three client-credentials app clients, the six dedicated IAM roles (`ecs_execution`, `ecs_task`, `projector`, `relay`, `archiver`, `scheduler`) with both least-privilege `Allow` statements and explicit `Effect = \"Deny\"` guardrails, the four customer-managed KMS keys and aliases (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`), and the four CloudWatch Log Groups (`retention_in_days >= 14`). - `/workspace/contracts/openapi.yaml`: HTTP endpoints, request and response bodies, required request headers (`Idempotency-Key`, `X-Correlation-Id`), and response headers (`X-ClearLedger-Source`, `X-ClearLedger-Version`, `X-ClearLedger-Instance`). - `/workspace/contracts/schemas/events.schema.json`: Domain event envelope schema used on SQS and in S3 NDJSON audit batches. - `/workspace/contracts/schemas/manifest.schema.json`: JSON Schema for `/workspace/submission/manifest.json` (`additionalProperties: false` throughout).  Deployment inputs (`resource_prefix`, `region`, `aws_endpoint_url`, `db_name`, `db_username`, `db_password`, and the four container image tags and digests: `api_image` / `clearledger/api:1.0.0`, `projector_image` / `clearledger/projector:1.0.0`, `relay_image` / `clearledger/relay:1.0.0`, `archiver_image` / `clearledger/archiver:1.0.0`) are stored in `/workspace/config/config.json`. Read those values dynamically in `deploy.sh` and `destroy.sh` instead of hardcoding values from the current workspace.  ## Deliverables and Lifecycle Rules  Write your solution under `/workspace/submission/`:  ```text /workspace/submission/ ????????? deploy.sh ????????? destroy.sh ????????? manifest.json ????????? infra/     ????????? *.tf (or *.tofu) ```  - `infra/`: Your Terraform or OpenTofu configuration. Deploy this as a container-based **AWS Lambda** function connected to the main SQS queue via an event source mapping (`batch_size = 5`, `ReportBatchItemFailures` enabled) backed by a dead-letter queue (`maxReceiveCount = 4`). 3. **Outbox relay (`relay_image`)**: Polls **Amazon RDS for PostgreSQL** for committed outbox rows (`published_at IS NULL`), publishes them to the main SQS queue, and stamps `published_at`. Deploy this as a container-based **AWS Lambda** function triggered every minute (`rate(1 minute)`) by **EventBridge Scheduler**. 4. **Audit archiver (`archiver_image`)**: Reads published settlement events from PostgreSQL (`published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`) and writes deterministic NDJSON audit batches (`ledger-audit/batch-*.ndjson`) to a private, versioned **Amazon S3** bucket. Deploy this as a container-based **AWS Lambda** function triggered every five minutes (`rate(5 minutes)`) by **EventBridge Scheduler**.  ## Workspace Environment and Contracts  The workspace comes with `terraform`, `tofu`, `aws`, `psql`, `jq`, `curl`, and Python 3 pre-installed. The local AWS control plane (`http://aws:4566`, region `us-east-1`) is configured through `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL` and `/workspace/config/config.json`. Docker is intentionally disabled inside the workspace; provision every cloud resource through Terraform or OpenTofu against the AWS endpoint.  Read the contract files under `/workspace/contracts/` before writing your code:  - `/workspace/contracts/runtime.md`: Container environment variables for the API and each Lambda worker (`DATABASE_URL`, `SQS_QUEUE_URL`, `PROJECTION_TABLE`, `VALKEY_URL`, `CACHE_TTL_SECONDS`, `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE`, `AUDIT_BUCKET`, `AUDIT_PREFIX`, `AUTH_ISSUER`, `AUTH_AUDIENCES`, `AUTH_JWKS_URL`, `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`), the three non-hierarchical Cognito OAuth2 scopes (`clearledger/read`, `clearledger/write`, `clearledger/admin`), and the data-plane reconciliation rules required when `deploy.sh` runs. - `/workspace/contracts/infrastructure.md` and `/workspace/contracts/services/*.md`: Service-by-service infrastructure requirements covering:   - `services/vpc.md`: Two-AZ VPC (`us-east-1a` and `us-east-1b`), public and private subnets, route tables, and four least-privilege security groups (`alb`, `ecs`, `rds`, `valkey`).   - `services/alb.md` and `services/ecs.md`: Application Load Balancer, target group, HTTP listener, ECS cluster (`containerInsights = \"enabled\"`), Fargate task definition, and service configuration.   - `services/rds.md`: RDS PostgreSQL 16 (`db.t4g.micro`) instance settings and the `clearledger` schema (`settlements`, `events`, `outbox`, `idempotency_keys`), `CHECK` / `UNIQUE` / `FOREIGN KEY` constraints, PL/pgSQL triggers, and partial/composite indexes.   - `services/sqs.md`, `services/lambda.md`, and `services/scheduler.md`: Main SQS queue and DLQ parameters, Lambda container workers, SQS event source mapping, and EventBridge Scheduler rules.   - `services/dynamodb.md`, `services/elasticache-valkey.md`, and `services/s3.md`: DynamoDB key schema (`PK`/`SK`), `AccountIndex` GSI (`GSI1PK`/`GSI1SK`), and point-in-time recovery, ElastiCache Valkey 8 (`cache.t4g.micro`), and S3 bucket versioning, KMS encryption, public-access block, and canonical batch key layout.   - `services/cognito.md`, `services/iam.md`, `services/kms.md`, and `services/cloudwatch-logs.md`: Cognito User Pool, resource server, and three client-credentials app clients, the six dedicated IAM roles (`ecs_execution`, `ecs_task`, `projector`, `relay`, `archiver`, `scheduler`) with both least-privilege `Allow` statements and explicit `Effect = \"Deny\"` guardrails, the four customer-managed KMS keys and aliases (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`), and the four CloudWatch Log Groups (`retention_in_days >= 14`). - `/workspace/contracts/openapi.yaml`: HTTP endpoints, request and response bodies, required request headers (`Idempotency-Key`, `X-Correlation-Id`), and response headers (`X-ClearLedger-Source`, `X-ClearLedger-Version`, `X-ClearLedger-Instance`). - `/workspace/contracts/schemas/events.schema.json`: Domain event envelope schema used on SQS and in S3 NDJSON audit batches. - `/workspace/contracts/schemas/manifest.schema.json`: JSON Schema for `/workspace/submission/manifest.json` (`additionalProperties: false` throughout).  Deployment inputs (`resource_prefix`, `region`, `aws_endpoint_url`, `db_name`, `db_username`, `db_password`, and the four container image tags and digests: `api_image` / `clearledger/api:1.0.0`, `projector_image` / `clearledger/projector:1.0.0`, `relay_image` / `clearledger/relay:1.0.0`, `archiver_image` / `clearledger/archiver:1.0.0`) are stored in `/workspace/config/config.json`. Read those values dynamically in `deploy.sh` and `destroy.sh` instead of hardcoding values from the current workspace.  ## Deliverables and Lifecycle Rules  Write your solution under `/workspace/submission/`:  ```text /workspace/submission/ ????????? deploy.sh ????????? destroy.sh ????????? manifest.json ????????? infra/     ????????? *.tf (or *.tofu) ```  - `infra/`: Your Terraform or OpenTofu configuration. All required cloud resources and IAM policies must be declared here and tracked in `/workspace/submission/infra/terraform.tfstate`. Do not create scored cloud resources via imperative `aws` CLI commands in `deploy.sh`. - `deploy.sh` (timeout: 720 seconds, max combined stdout/stderr: 8 MiB):   - Initializes and applies `/workspace/submission/infra` with local state at `/workspace/submission/infra/terraform.tfstate`.   - Idempotently creates the `clearledger` PostgreSQL schema, tables, relational `CHECK` / `UNIQUE` / `FOREIGN KEY` constraints, triggers, and indexes on the RDS instance as specified in `/workspace/contracts/services/rds.md`.   - Exports `/workspace/submission/manifest.json` (max 1 MiB) from your Terraform/OpenTofu outputs matching `/workspace/contracts/schemas/manifest.schema.json`.   - Waits until `GET <service_url>/health/ready` returns HTTP `200`.   - Handles repair and re-apply cleanly: re-running `deploy.sh` must keep the existing RDS instance, DynamoDB table, and S3 bucket intact without losing committed data, fix out-of-band control-plane drift (across SQS/DLQ attributes, EventBridge schedules, CloudWatch Log Group retention, Lambda environment variables, out-of-band inline or attached IAM policies on the six workload roles, and security group egress rules), recreate and re-wire any deleted resources (such as the SQS queues, schedules, or the projector event source mapping), drain unpublished outbox rows (`published_at IS NULL`) through `outbox_relay`, reconcile DynamoDB (`STATE`, `EVENT#*`, and `AccountIndex` GSI attributes) and Valkey cache entries 1-to-1 against `clearledger.settlements` and `clearledger.events` (deleting any orphan partitions or stray items/keys), and reconcile the versioned S3 `ledger-audit/` archive 1-to-1 against `clearledger.outbox` through `audit_archiver` (including purging noncurrent object `Versions` and `DeleteMarkers`) before exiting `0`. -
